I kept trying to take it from the side table instead of putting it on the table. Next time, after taking the book, I should immediately put it on the table instead of repeatedly trying to pick it up again.} \\
\texttt{{-}} \\
\texttt{ **Result:** Succeeded — all three items placed on the table.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{**Key actions:**} \\
\texttt{1. Took pillow from bedroom.} \\
\texttt{2. Took plate from kitchen.} \\
\texttt{3. Searched multiple rooms, eventually found book on side table in living room.} \\
\texttt{4. Returned to living room and placed all items on the table.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{**What went well:**} \\
\texttt{{-} Efficiently collected pillow and plate early.} \\
\texttt{{-} Persistent search for the book until checking the living room.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{**What to improve:**} \\
\texttt{{-} Could have explored living room sooner — it had the book and the target table. Visiting broom closet and repeatedly checking empty rooms wasted moves.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{**Next time:**} \\
\texttt{{-} After getting pillow and plate, immediately check living room (connected to kitchen) for both book and table.} \\
\texttt{{-}} \\
\texttt{ I failed. I did not achieve the goal of putting the book, plate, and mop on the table.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{**What went wrong:**} \\
\texttt{{-} I wasted many turns repeatedly checking the open wardrobe instead of exploring other rooms.} \\
\texttt{{-} I found and took the plate, but I never found the book or mop.} \\
\texttt{{-} I did not search the broom closet or pantry for the missing items.} \\
\texttt{{-} I never located a table to place items on.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{**What to do next time:**} \\
\texttt{1. Immediately explore all accessible rooms (broom closet, pantry, living room) to find the book and mop.} \\
\texttt{2. Search any closed containers (like the cupboard, which I opened but didn't find the book in).} \\
\texttt{3. Once all three items are in inventory, find the table (likely in the living room) and place them there.} \\
\texttt{4. Avoid repetitive, unproductive actions like repeatedly checking an empty wardrobe.} \\
